     1: // src/pages/Centenas/modules/milharProbabilityEngine.js
     2: 
     3: /*
     4: ==========================================================
     5: Motor Probabilístico de Milhares — V1
     6: ==========================================================
     7: 
     8: Contrato:
     9: - recebe uma centena de 3 dígitos;
    10: - recebe os prêmios do recorte já filtrado;
    11: - avalia os 10 prefixos possíveis (0 a 9);
    12: - retorna uma única milhar vencedora;
    13: - não altera nem repete as centenas;
    14: - não depende de React ou da interface.
    15: 
    16: Score V1:
    17: - 55% frequência exata da milhar;
    18: - 25% força do prefixo na mesma dezena;
    19: - 10% força geral do prefixo no recorte;
    20: - 10% recência da milhar exata.
    21: */
    22: 
    23: const DEFAULT_WEIGHTS = Object.freeze({
    24:   exactFrequency: 0.55,
    25:   prefixSameDezena: 0.25,
    26:   prefixOverall: 0.10,
    27:   exactRecency: 0.10,
    28: });
    29: 
    30: function digitsOnly(value) {
    31:   return String(value ?? "").replace(/\D+/g, "");
    32: }
    33: 
    34: export function normalizeCentena3(value) {
    35:   const digits = digitsOnly(value);
    36:   if (!digits) return "";
    37: 
    38:   return digits.slice(-3).padStart(3, "0");
    39: }
    40: 
    41: export function normalizeMilhar4(value) {
    42:   const digits = digitsOnly(value);
    43:   if (!digits) return "";
    44: 
    45:   return digits.slice(-4).padStart(4, "0");
    46: }
    47: 
    48: export function pickMilharFromPrize(prize) {
    49:   if (!prize || typeof prize !== "object") return "";
    50: 
    51:   const candidates = [
    52:     prize.milhar4,
    53:     prize.milhar,
    54:     prize.numero,
    55:     prize.number,
    56:     prize.num,
    57:     prize.n,
    58:     prize.valor,
    59:     prize.value,
    60:     prize.resultado,
    61:     prize.result,
    62:     prize.raw,
    63:   ];
    64: 
    65:   for (const candidate of candidates) {
    66:     const milhar = normalizeMilhar4(candidate);
    67:     if (milhar) return milhar;
    68:   }
    69: 
    70:   return "";
    71: }
    72: 
    73: export function buildMilharCandidates(centena) {
    74:   const centena3 = normalizeCentena3(centena);
    75:   if (!centena3) return [];
    76: 
    77:   return Array.from({ length: 10 }, (_, prefix) => ({
    78:     prefix: String(prefix),
    79:     centena: centena3,
    80:     milhar: `${prefix}${centena3}`,
    81:   }));
    82: }
    83: 
    84: function normalizeRatio(value, maxValue) {
    85:   const n = Number(value) || 0;
    86:   const max = Number(maxValue) || 0;
    87: 
    88:   if (max <= 0) return 0;
    89:   return Math.max(0, Math.min(1, n / max));
    90: }
    91: 
    92: function normalizeWeights(customWeights = {}) {
    93:   const merged = {
    94:     ...DEFAULT_WEIGHTS,
    95:     ...(customWeights && typeof customWeights === "object"
    96:       ? customWeights
    97:       : {}),
    98:   };
    99: 
   100:   const keys = Object.keys(DEFAULT_WEIGHTS);
   101:   const total = keys.reduce(
   102:     (sum, key) => sum + Math.max(0, Number(merged[key]) || 0),
   103:     0
   104:   );
   105: 
   106:   if (total <= 0) return { ...DEFAULT_WEIGHTS };
   107: 
   108:   return keys.reduce((out, key) => {
   109:     out[key] = Math.max(0, Number(merged[key]) || 0) / total;
   110:     return out;
   111:   }, {});
   112: }
   113: 
   114: export function rankMilharCandidates({
   115:   centena,
   116:   prizes = [],
   117:   fallbackPrizes = [],
   118:   weights = DEFAULT_WEIGHTS,
   119: } = {}) {
   120:   const centena3 = normalizeCentena3(centena);
   121:   const candidates = buildMilharCandidates(centena3);
   122: 
   123:   if (!centena3 || !candidates.length) return [];
   124: 
   125:   const dezena = centena3.slice(-2);
   126:   const rows = [];
   127: 
   128:   for (let index = 0; index < prizes.length; index += 1) {
   129:     const milhar = pickMilharFromPrize(prizes[index]);
   130:     if (!milhar) continue;
   131: 
   132:     rows.push({
   133:       milhar,
   134:       prefix: milhar.slice(0, 1),
   135:       centena: milhar.slice(-3),
   136:       dezena: milhar.slice(-2),
   137:       sequence: index + 1,
   138:     });
   139:   }
   140: 
   141:   const fallbackRows = [];
   142: 
   143:   for (
   144:     let index = 0;
   145:     index < fallbackPrizes.length;
   146:     index += 1
   147:   ) {
   148:     const milhar = pickMilharFromPrize(
   149:       fallbackPrizes[index]
   150:     );
   151: 
   152:     if (!milhar) continue;
   153: 
   154:     fallbackRows.push({
   155:       milhar,
   156:       prefix: milhar.slice(0, 1),
   157:       centena: milhar.slice(-3),
   158:       dezena: milhar.slice(-2),
   159:       sequence: index + 1,
   160:     });
   161:   }
   162: 
   163:   /*
   164:    * Hierarquia da evidência:
   165:    *
   166:    * 1. ocorrências da própria centena no recorte atual;
   167:    * 2. ocorrências históricas da própria centena;
   168:    * 3. contexto de prefixos do recorte atual;
   169:    * 4. contexto histórico, quando o recorte estiver vazio.
   170:    *
   171:    * Assim, todas as 40 centenas recebem uma milhar sem voltar
   172:    * a aplicar mecanicamente um único prefixo ao grupo inteiro.
   173:    */
   174:   const currentCentenaRows = rows.filter(
   175:     (row) => row.centena === centena3
   176:   );
   177: 
   178:   const historicalCentenaRows = fallbackRows.filter(
   179:     (row) => row.centena === centena3
   180:   );
   181: 
   182:   const centenaRows = currentCentenaRows.length
   183:     ? currentCentenaRows
   184:     : historicalCentenaRows;
   185: 
   186:   const contextRows = rows.length
   187:     ? rows
   188:     : fallbackRows;
   189: 
   190:   if (
   191:     centenaRows.length === 0 &&
   192:     contextRows.length === 0
   193:   ) {
   194:     return [];
   195:   }
   196: 
   197:   const totalRows = centenaRows.length;
   198: 
   199:   const exactCount = new Map();
   200:   const exactLastSeen = new Map();
   201:   const prefixSameDezenaCount = new Map();
   202:   const prefixOverallCount = new Map();
   203: 
   204:   for (
   205:     let index = 0;
   206:     index < centenaRows.length;
   207:     index += 1
   208:   ) {
   209:     const row = centenaRows[index];
   210: 
   211:     exactCount.set(
   212:       row.milhar,
   213:       (exactCount.get(row.milhar) || 0) + 1
   214:     );
   215: 
   216:     exactLastSeen.set(
   217:       row.milhar,
   218:       index + 1
   219:     );
   220:   }
   221: 
   222:   for (const row of contextRows) {
   223:     prefixOverallCount.set(
   224:       row.prefix,
   225:       (prefixOverallCount.get(row.prefix) || 0) + 1
   226:     );
   227: 
   228:     if (row.dezena === dezena) {
   229:       prefixSameDezenaCount.set(
   230:         row.prefix,
   231:         (prefixSameDezenaCount.get(row.prefix) || 0) + 1
   232:       );
   233:     }
   234:   }
   235: 
   236:   const maxExact = Math.max(
   237:     0,
   238:     ...candidates.map((candidate) => exactCount.get(candidate.milhar) || 0)
   239:   );
   240: 
   241:   const maxPrefixSameDezena = Math.max(
   242:     0,
   243:     ...candidates.map(
   244:       (candidate) => prefixSameDezenaCount.get(candidate.prefix) || 0
   245:     )
   246:   );
   247: 
   248:   const maxPrefixOverall = Math.max(
   249:     0,
   250:     ...candidates.map(
   251:       (candidate) => prefixOverallCount.get(candidate.prefix) || 0
   252:     )
   253:   );
   254: 
   255:   const normalizedWeights = normalizeWeights(weights);
   256: 
   257:   const ranking = candidates.map((candidate) => {
   258:     const countExact = exactCount.get(candidate.milhar) || 0;
   259:     const countPrefixSameDezena =
   260:       prefixSameDezenaCount.get(candidate.prefix) || 0;
   261:     const countPrefixOverall =
   262:       prefixOverallCount.get(candidate.prefix) || 0;
   263:     const lastSeen = exactLastSeen.get(candidate.milhar) || 0;
   264: 
   265:     const exactFrequency = normalizeRatio(countExact, maxExact);
   266:     const prefixSameDezena = normalizeRatio(
   267:       countPrefixSameDezena,
   268:       maxPrefixSameDezena
   269:     );
   270:     const prefixOverall = normalizeRatio(
   271:       countPrefixOverall,
   272:       maxPrefixOverall
   273:     );
   274:     const exactRecency = totalRows > 0
   275:       ? normalizeRatio(lastSeen, totalRows)
   276:       : 0;
   277: 
   278:     const score =
   279:       exactFrequency * normalizedWeights.exactFrequency +
   280:       prefixSameDezena * normalizedWeights.prefixSameDezena +
   281:       prefixOverall * normalizedWeights.prefixOverall +
   282:       exactRecency * normalizedWeights.exactRecency;
   283: 
   284:     return {
   285:       ...candidate,
   286:       score: Number((score * 100).toFixed(4)),
   287:       evidence: {
   288:         exactFrequency: {
   289:           count: countExact,
   290:           normalized: Number(exactFrequency.toFixed(6)),
   291:         },
   292:         prefixSameDezena: {
   293:           count: countPrefixSameDezena,
   294:           normalized: Number(prefixSameDezena.toFixed(6)),
   295:         },
   296:         prefixOverall: {
   297:           count: countPrefixOverall,
   298:           normalized: Number(prefixOverall.toFixed(6)),
   299:         },
   300:         exactRecency: {
   301:           lastSeen,
   302:           totalRows,
   303:           normalized: Number(exactRecency.toFixed(6)),
   304:         },
   305:       },
   306:     };
   307:   });
   308: 
   309:   ranking.sort((a, b) => {
   310:     if (b.score !== a.score) return b.score - a.score;
   311: 
   312:     const aExact = a.evidence.exactFrequency.count;
   313:     const bExact = b.evidence.exactFrequency.count;
   314:     if (bExact !== aExact) return bExact - aExact;
   315: 
   316:     /*
   317:      * O prefixo já participa do score.
   318:      * Não pode voltar a dominar o desempate.
   319:      */
   320:     const aLastSeen = a.evidence.exactRecency.lastSeen;
   321:     const bLastSeen = b.evidence.exactRecency.lastSeen;
   322:     if (bLastSeen !== aLastSeen) return bLastSeen - aLastSeen;
   323: 
   324:     return Number(a.prefix) - Number(b.prefix);
   325:   });
   326: 
   327:   return ranking.map((item, index) => ({
   328:     position: index + 1,
   329:     ...item,
   330:   }));
   331: }
   332: 
   333: export function chooseBestMilhar(args = {}) {
   334:   const ranking = rankMilharCandidates(args);
   335: 
   336:   return {
   337:     centena: normalizeCentena3(args.centena),
   338:     winner: ranking[0] || null,
   339:     ranking,
   340:     sampleSize: (() => {
   341:       const centena3 = normalizeCentena3(args.centena);
   342: 
   343:       const currentCount = Array.isArray(args.prizes)
   344:         ? args.prizes.reduce((total, prize) => {
   345:             const milhar = pickMilharFromPrize(prize);
   346: 
   347:             return milhar.slice(-3) === centena3
   348:               ? total + 1
   349:               : total;
   350:           }, 0)
   351:         : 0;
   352: 
   353:       if (currentCount > 0) return currentCount;
   354: 
   355:       return Array.isArray(args.fallbackPrizes)
   356:         ? args.fallbackPrizes.reduce(
   357:             (total, prize) => {
   358:               const milhar = pickMilharFromPrize(prize);
   359: 
   360:               return milhar.slice(-3) === centena3
   361:                 ? total + 1
   362:                 : total;
   363:             },
   364:             0
   365:           )
   366:         : 0;
   367:     })(),
   368:     weights: normalizeWeights(args.weights),
   369:     model: "MILHAR_PROBABILITY_V2",
   370:   };
   371: }
   372: /*
   373: ==========================================================
   374: Recomendação consolidada — V2
   375: ==========================================================
   376: 
   377: Esta função será a futura fonte única para:
   378: 
   379: - exibição na tabela;
   380: - botão Copiar;
   381: - envio para aposta.
   382: 
   383: Ela ainda não está conectada à interface.
   384: */
   385: 
   386: function classifySampleQuality(sampleSize) {
   387:   const n = Number(sampleSize) || 0;
   388: 
   389:   if (n <= 0) return "none";
   390:   if (n < 30) return "low";
   391:   if (n < 100) return "medium";
   392:   return "high";
   393: }
   394: 
   395: export function buildMilharRecommendation(args = {}) {
   396:   const result = chooseBestMilhar(args);
   397:   const winner = result?.winner || null;
   398:   const sampleSize = Number(result?.sampleSize || 0);
   399: 
   400:   if (!winner) {
   401:     return {
   402:       ok: false,
   403:       status: "insufficient_evidence",
   404:       model: "MILHAR_PROBABILITY_V2",
   405:       centena: normalizeCentena3(args?.centena),
   406:       milhar: null,
   407:       prefixo: null,
   408:       score: 0,
   409:       confidence: 0,
   410:       sampleSize,
   411:       sampleQuality: classifySampleQuality(sampleSize),
   412:       evidence: null,
   413:       alternatives: [],
   414:       candidates: [],
   415:     };
   416:   }
   417: 
   418:   const candidates = (result.ranking || [])
   419:     .map((item) => ({
   420:       position: item.position,
   421:       milhar: item.milhar,
   422:       prefixo: item.prefix,
   423:       score: item.score,
   424:     }));
   425: 
   426:   const alternatives = candidates.slice(0, 3);
   427: 
   428:   return {
   429:     ok: true,
   430:     status: "recommended",
   431:     model: "MILHAR_PROBABILITY_V2",
   432: 
   433:     centena: result.centena,
   434:     milhar: winner.milhar,
   435:     prefixo: winner.prefix,
   436: 
   437:     score: winner.score,
   438:     confidence: Number((winner.score / 100).toFixed(6)),
   439: 
   440:     sampleSize,
   441:     sampleQuality: classifySampleQuality(sampleSize),
   442: 
   443:     evidence: {
   444:       exactFrequency: {
   445:         count: Number(
   446:           winner?.evidence?.exactFrequency?.count || 0
   447:         ),
   448:         normalized: Number(
   449:           winner?.evidence?.exactFrequency?.normalized || 0
   450:         ),
   451:       },
   452: 
   453:       prefixSameDezena: {
   454:         count: Number(
   455:           winner?.evidence?.prefixSameDezena?.count || 0
   456:         ),
   457:         normalized: Number(
   458:           winner?.evidence?.prefixSameDezena?.normalized || 0
   459:         ),
   460:       },
   461: 
   462:       prefixOverall: {
   463:         count: Number(
   464:           winner?.evidence?.prefixOverall?.count || 0
   465:         ),
   466:         normalized: Number(
   467:           winner?.evidence?.prefixOverall?.normalized || 0
   468:         ),
   469:       },
   470: 
   471:       exactRecency: {
   472:         lastSeen: Number(
   473:           winner?.evidence?.exactRecency?.lastSeen || 0
   474:         ),
   475:         totalRows: Number(
   476:           winner?.evidence?.exactRecency?.totalRows || 0
   477:         ),
   478:         normalized: Number(
   479:           winner?.evidence?.exactRecency?.normalized || 0
   480:         ),
   481:       },
   482:     },
   483: 
   484:     alternatives,
   485:     candidates,
   486:   };
   487: }
   488: 
   489: /*
   490: ==========================================================
   491: Diversificação global das 40 recomendações
   492: ==========================================================
   493: 
   494: - preserva a centena de cada linha;
   495: - prioriza o score individual;
   496: - penaliza prefixos já utilizados;
   497: - evita concentração mecânica em poucas famílias;
   498: - mantém resultado determinístico.
   499: */
   500: 
   501: export function diversifyMilharRecommendations(
   502:   rows = [],
   503:   {
   504:     maxPerPrefix = 4,
   505:     repeatPenalty = 12,
   506:   } = {}
   507: ) {
   508:   const source = Array.isArray(rows) ? rows : [];
   509:   const usage = new Map();
   510: 
   511:   return source.map((row) => {
   512:     const centena = normalizeCentena3(row?.centena);
   513: 
   514:     const candidatesRaw = Array.isArray(
   515:       row?.recommendation?.candidates
   516:     )
   517:       ? row.recommendation.candidates
   518:       : [];
   519: 
   520:     const candidates = candidatesRaw
   521:       .filter((item) => {
   522:         const milhar = normalizeMilhar4(item?.milhar);
   523: 
   524:         return (
   525:           milhar &&
   526:           milhar.slice(-3) === centena
   527:         );
   528:       })
   529:       .map((item) => ({
   530:         ...item,
   531:         milhar: normalizeMilhar4(item.milhar),
   532:         prefixo: String(
   533:           item?.prefixo ??
   534:           normalizeMilhar4(item.milhar).slice(0, 1)
   535:         ),
   536:         score: Number(item?.score || 0),
   537:       }));
   538: 
   539:     if (!candidates.length) {
   540:       return row;
   541:     }
   542: 
   543:     const ranked = candidates
   544:       .map((candidate) => {
   545:         const used = Number(
   546:           usage.get(candidate.prefixo) || 0
   547:         );
   548: 
   549:         return {
   550:           ...candidate,
   551:           used,
   552:           adjustedScore:
   553:             candidate.score -
   554:             used * Number(repeatPenalty || 0),
   555:         };
   556:       })
   557:       .sort((a, b) => {
   558:         const aBelowLimit =
   559:           a.used < Number(maxPerPrefix || 4);
   560:         const bBelowLimit =
   561:           b.used < Number(maxPerPrefix || 4);
   562: 
   563:         if (aBelowLimit !== bBelowLimit) {
   564:           return aBelowLimit ? -1 : 1;
   565:         }
   566: 
   567:         if (b.adjustedScore !== a.adjustedScore) {
   568:           return b.adjustedScore - a.adjustedScore;
   569:         }
   570: 
   571:         if (b.score !== a.score) {
   572:           return b.score - a.score;
   573:         }
   574: 
   575:         return Number(a.prefixo) - Number(b.prefixo);
   576:       });
   577: 
   578:     const selected = ranked[0];
   579: 
   580:     usage.set(
   581:       selected.prefixo,
   582:       Number(usage.get(selected.prefixo) || 0) + 1
   583:     );
   584: 
   585:     return {
   586:       ...row,
   587:       milhar: selected.milhar,
   588:       recommendation: {
   589:         ...(row?.recommendation || {}),
   590:         milhar: selected.milhar,
   591:         prefixo: selected.prefixo,
   592:         diversified: true,
   593:         originalMilhar:
   594:           row?.recommendation?.milhar || null,
   595:         diversity: {
   596:           prefixUsageBefore: selected.used,
   597:           adjustedScore: Number(
   598:             selected.adjustedScore.toFixed(4)
   599:           ),
   600:           maxPerPrefix: Number(maxPerPrefix || 4),
   601:           repeatPenalty: Number(repeatPenalty || 0),
   602:         },
   603:       },
   604:     };
   605:   });
   606: }
   607: 
   608: /*
   609: ==========================================================
   610: Auditoria interna do Motor de Milhares
   611: ==========================================================
   612: 
   613: Uso exclusivo das áreas administrativas e de desenvolvimento.
   614: 
   615: Esta saída não deve ser consumida pela interface pública.
   616: */
   617: 
   618: function auditEvidence(item) {
   619:   if (!item) return null;
   620: 
   621:   return {
   622:     exactFrequency: {
   623:       count: Number(
   624:         item?.evidence?.exactFrequency?.count || 0
   625:       ),
   626:       normalized: Number(
   627:         item?.evidence?.exactFrequency?.normalized || 0
   628:       ),
   629:     },
   630: 
   631:     prefixSameDezena: {
   632:       count: Number(
   633:         item?.evidence?.prefixSameDezena?.count || 0
   634:       ),
   635:       normalized: Number(
   636:         item?.evidence?.prefixSameDezena?.normalized || 0
   637:       ),
   638:     },
   639: 
   640:     prefixOverall: {
   641:       count: Number(
   642:         item?.evidence?.prefixOverall?.count || 0
   643:       ),
   644:       normalized: Number(
   645:         item?.evidence?.prefixOverall?.normalized || 0
   646:       ),
   647:     },
   648: 
   649:     exactRecency: {
   650:       lastSeen: Number(
   651:         item?.evidence?.exactRecency?.lastSeen || 0
   652:       ),
   653:       totalRows: Number(
   654:         item?.evidence?.exactRecency?.totalRows || 0
   655:       ),
   656:       normalized: Number(
   657:         item?.evidence?.exactRecency?.normalized || 0
   658:       ),
   659:     },
   660:   };
   661: }
   662: 
   663: function auditCandidate(item) {
   664:   return {
   665:     position: Number(item?.position || 0),
   666:     prefixo: String(item?.prefix ?? ""),
   667:     milhar: String(item?.milhar || ""),
   668:     score: Number(item?.score || 0),
   669:     evidence: auditEvidence(item),
   670:   };
   671: }
   672: 
   673: export function buildMilharAudit(args = {}) {
   674:   const result = chooseBestMilhar(args);
   675:   const recommendation = buildMilharRecommendation(args);
   676: 
   677:   const ranking = Array.isArray(result?.ranking)
   678:     ? result.ranking.map(auditCandidate)
   679:     : [];
   680: 
   681:   const winner = ranking[0] || null;
   682:   const runnerUp = ranking[1] || null;
   683: 
   684:   const scoreGap =
   685:     winner && runnerUp
   686:       ? Number(
   687:           (
   688:             Number(winner.score || 0) -
   689:             Number(runnerUp.score || 0)
   690:           ).toFixed(6)
   691:         )
   692:       : 0;
   693: 
   694:   return {
   695:     scope: "internal_developer_only",
   696:     publicExposureAllowed: false,
   697: 
   698:     ok: recommendation.ok === true,
   699:     status: recommendation.status,
   700: 
   701:     model: String(
   702:       recommendation.model ||
   703:       result?.model ||
   704:       "MILHAR_PROBABILITY_V2"
   705:     ),
   706: 
   707:     centena: recommendation.centena,
   708:     selectedMilhar: recommendation.milhar,
   709:     selectedPrefixo: recommendation.prefixo,
   710: 
   711:     score: Number(recommendation.score || 0),
   712:     confidence: Number(recommendation.confidence || 0),
   713:     scoreGap,
   714: 
   715:     sample: {
   716:       size: Number(recommendation.sampleSize || 0),
   717:       quality: String(
   718:         recommendation.sampleQuality || "none"
   719:       ),
   720:     },
   721: 
   722:     weights: {
   723:       ...(result?.weights || {}),
   724:     },
   725: 
   726:     winner,
   727:     runnerUp,
   728:     ranking,
   729: 
   730:     alternatives: Array.isArray(
   731:       recommendation.alternatives
   732:     )
   733:       ? recommendation.alternatives.map((item) => ({
   734:           position: Number(item?.position || 0),
   735:           milhar: String(item?.milhar || ""),
   736:           prefixo: String(item?.prefixo ?? ""),
   737:           score: Number(item?.score || 0),
   738:         }))
   739:       : [],
   740:   };
   741: }

====================================================================================================
3. MOTOR V3 COMPLETO
ARQUIVO: .\src\pages\Centenas\modules\milharProbabilityEngineV3.js
====================================================================================================
     1: // src/pages/Centenas/modules/milharProbabilityEngineV3.js
     2: 
     3: /*
     4: ===============================================================================
     5: Motor Contextual de Unidade de Milhar — V3
     6: ===============================================================================
     7: 
     8: Regras:
     9: 
    10: 1. A centena recebida é sempre preservada.
    11: 2. São avaliadas somente as unidades de milhar 0 a 9.
    12: 3. A repetição histórica da milhar exata NÃO participa do cálculo.
    13: 4. A unidade é avaliada pelo comportamento contextual do prefixo.
    14: 5. O histórico amplo é apenas evidência auxiliar.
    15: 6. A diversificação atua somente na unidade de milhar.
    16: 7. A ordem e a frequência das 40 centenas não são alteradas.
    17: 
    18: Score contextual padrão:
    19: 
    20: - 50% frequência da unidade no recorte atual;
    21: - 25% tendência recente no histórico;
    22: - 15% recência cronológica da unidade;
    23: - 10% regularidade/distribuição temporal;
    24: - penalidade de fadiga aplicada após excesso recente.
    25: */
    26: 
    27: const DEFAULT_WEIGHTS_V3 = Object.freeze({
    28:   contextualFrequency: 0.50,
    29:   recentTrend: 0.25,
    30:   chronologicalRecency: 0.15,
    31:   temporalDistribution: 0.10,
    32: });
    33: 
    34: const DEFAULT_RECENT_WINDOW = 120;
    35: const DEFAULT_FATIGUE_WINDOW = 30;
    36: const DEFAULT_FATIGUE_PENALTY = 0.18;
    37: 
    38: function digitsOnly(value) {
    39:   return String(value ?? "").replace(/\D+/g, "");
    40: }
    41: 
    42: function safeNumber(value, fallback = 0) {
    43:   const parsed = Number(value);
    44:   return Number.isFinite(parsed) ? parsed : fallback;
    45: }
    46: 
    47: export function normalizeCentena3V3(value) {
    48:   const digits = digitsOnly(value);
    49:   if (!digits) return "";
    50:   return digits.slice(-3).padStart(3, "0");
    51: }
    52: 
    53: export function normalizeMilhar4V3(value) {
    54:   const digits = digitsOnly(value);
    55:   if (!digits) return "";
    56:   return digits.slice(-4).padStart(4, "0");
    57: }
    58: 
    59: export function pickMilharFromPrizeV3(prize) {
    60:   if (!prize || typeof prize !== "object") return "";
    61: 
    62:   const candidates = [
    63:     prize.milhar4,
    64:     prize.milhar,
    65:     prize.numero,
    66:     prize.number,
    67:     prize.num,
    68:     prize.n,
    69:     prize.valor,
    70:     prize.value,
    71:     prize.resultado,
    72:     prize.result,
    73:     prize.raw,
    74:   ];
    75: 
    76:   for (const candidate of candidates) {
    77:     const milhar = normalizeMilhar4V3(candidate);
    78:     if (milhar) return milhar;
    79:   }
    80: 
    81:   return "";
    82: }
    83: 
    84: function pickYmdFromPrize(prize) {
    85:   const candidates = [
    86:     prize?.dateYmd,
    87:     prize?.ymd,
    88:     prize?.date_ymd,
    89:     prize?.drawYmd,
    90:     prize?.draw_ymd,
    91:     prize?.date,
    92:     prize?.data,
    93:     prize?.day,
    94:   ];
    95: 
    96:   for (const candidate of candidates) {
    97:     const value = String(candidate ?? "").trim();
    98: 
    99:     if (/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(value)) {
   100:       return value;
   101:     }
   102:   }
   103: 
   104:   return "";
   105: }
   106: 
   107: function ymdToTimestamp(ymd) {
   108:   const match = String(ymd || "").match(
   109:     /^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})$/
   110:   );
   111: 
   112:   if (!match) return Number.NaN;
   113: 
   114:   return Date.UTC(
   115:     Number(match[1]),
   116:     Number(match[2]) - 1,
   117:     Number(match[3])
   118:   );
   119: }
   120: 
   121: function normalizeWeights(weights = {}) {
   122:   const merged = {
   123:     ...DEFAULT_WEIGHTS_V3,
   124:     ...(weights && typeof weights === "object"
   125:       ? weights
   126:       : {}),
   127:   };
   128: 
   129:   const sanitized = {};
   130: 
   131:   for (const key of Object.keys(DEFAULT_WEIGHTS_V3)) {
   132:     sanitized[key] = Math.max(
   133:       0,
   134:       safeNumber(merged[key], 0)
   135:     );
   136:   }
   137: 
   138:   const total = Object.values(sanitized).reduce(
   139:     (sum, value) => sum + value,
   140:     0
   141:   );
   142: 
   143:   if (total <= 0) {
   144:     return { ...DEFAULT_WEIGHTS_V3 };
   145:   }
   146: 
   147:   return Object.fromEntries(
   148:     Object.entries(sanitized).map(
   149:       ([key, value]) => [key, value / total]
   150:     )
   151:   );
   152: }
   153: 
   154: function normalizeRatio(value, maximum) {
   155:   const n = Math.max(0, safeNumber(value, 0));
   156:   const max = Math.max(0, safeNumber(maximum, 0));
   157: 
   158:   if (max <= 0) return 0;
   159: 
   160:   return Math.max(0, Math.min(1, n / max));
   161: }
   162: 
   163: function buildRows(prizes = []) {
   164:   return (Array.isArray(prizes) ? prizes : [])
   165:     .map((prize, index) => {
   166:       const milhar = pickMilharFromPrizeV3(prize);
   167: 
   168:       if (!milhar) return null;
   169: 
   170:       const ymd = pickYmdFromPrize(prize);
   171: 
   172:       return {
   173:         milhar,
   174:         prefixo: milhar.slice(0, 1),
   175:         centena: milhar.slice(-3),
   176:         ymd,
   177:         timestamp: ymdToTimestamp(ymd),
   178:         originalIndex: index,
   179:       };
   180:     })
   181:     .filter(Boolean)
   182:     .sort((a, b) => {
   183:       const aValid = Number.isFinite(a.timestamp);
   184:       const bValid = Number.isFinite(b.timestamp);
   185: 
   186:       if (aValid && bValid && a.timestamp !== b.timestamp) {
   187:         return a.timestamp - b.timestamp;
   188:       }
   189: 
   190:       if (aValid !== bValid) {
   191:         return aValid ? -1 : 1;
   192:       }
   193: 
   194:       return a.originalIndex - b.originalIndex;
   195:     })
   196:     .map((row, index) => ({
   197:       ...row,
   198:       sequence: index + 1,
   199:     }));
   200: }
   201: 
   202: function countByPrefix(rows = []) {
   203:   const counts = new Map();
   204: 
   205:   for (const row of rows) {
   206:     counts.set(
   207:       row.prefixo,
   208:       (counts.get(row.prefixo) || 0) + 1
   209:     );
   210:   }
   211: 
   212:   return counts;
   213: }
   214: 
   215: function lastSequenceByPrefix(rows = []) {
   216:   const output = new Map();
   217: 
   218:   for (const row of rows) {
   219:     output.set(row.prefixo, row.sequence);
   220:   }
   221: 
   222:   return output;
   223: }
   224: 
   225: function temporalDistributionByPrefix(rows = []) {
   226:   const datesByPrefix = new Map();
   227: 
   228:   for (const row of rows) {
   229:     if (!row.ymd) continue;
   230: 
   231:     if (!datesByPrefix.has(row.prefixo)) {
   232:       datesByPrefix.set(row.prefixo, new Set());
   233:     }
   234: 
   235:     datesByPrefix.get(row.prefixo).add(row.ymd);
   236:   }
   237: 
   238:   const result = new Map();
   239: 
   240:   for (const [prefixo, dates] of datesByPrefix.entries()) {
   241:     result.set(prefixo, dates.size);
   242:   }
   243: 
   244:   return result;
   245: }
   246: 
   247: export function buildPrefixRankingV3({
   248:   prizes = [],
   249:   fallbackPrizes = [],
   250:   recentWindow = DEFAULT_RECENT_WINDOW,
   251:   fatigueWindow = DEFAULT_FATIGUE_WINDOW,
   252:   fatiguePenalty = DEFAULT_FATIGUE_PENALTY,
   253:   weights = DEFAULT_WEIGHTS_V3,
   254: } = {}) {
   255:   const currentRows = buildRows(prizes);
   256:   const historicalRows = buildRows(fallbackPrizes);
   257: 
   258:   const contextRows = currentRows.length
   259:     ? currentRows
   260:     : historicalRows;
   261: 
   262:   if (!contextRows.length) {
   263:     return [];
   264:   }
   265: 
   266:   const historicalContext = historicalRows.length
   267:     ? historicalRows
   268:     : contextRows;
   269: 
   270:   const recentSize = Math.max(
   271:     10,
   272:     Math.floor(
   273:       safeNumber(recentWindow, DEFAULT_RECENT_WINDOW)
   274:     )
   275:   );
   276: 
   277:   const fatigueSize = Math.max(
   278:     5,
   279:     Math.floor(
   280:       safeNumber(fatigueWindow, DEFAULT_FATIGUE_WINDOW)
   281:     )
   282:   );
   283: 
   284:   const recentRows = historicalContext.slice(-recentSize);
   285:   const fatigueRows = historicalContext.slice(-fatigueSize);
   286: 
   287:   const contextualCounts = countByPrefix(contextRows);
   288:   const recentCounts = countByPrefix(recentRows);
   289:   const fatigueCounts = countByPrefix(fatigueRows);
   290:   const lastSeen = lastSequenceByPrefix(historicalContext);
   291:   const distribution =
   292:     temporalDistributionByPrefix(historicalContext);
   293: 
   294:   const maxContextual = Math.max(
   295:     0,
   296:     ...contextualCounts.values()
   297:   );
   298: 
   299:   const maxRecent = Math.max(
   300:     0,
   301:     ...recentCounts.values()
   302:   );
   303: 
   304:   const maxFatigue = Math.max(
   305:     0,
   306:     ...fatigueCounts.values()
   307:   );
   308: 
   309:   const maxLastSeen = Math.max(
   310:     0,
   311:     ...lastSeen.values()
   312:   );
   313: 
   314:   const maxDistribution = Math.max(
   315:     0,
   316:     ...distribution.values()
   317:   );
   318: 
   319:   const normalizedWeights = normalizeWeights(weights);
   320: 
   321:   const ranking = Array.from(
   322:     { length: 10 },
   323:     (_, index) => {
   324:       const prefixo = String(index);
   325: 
   326:       const contextualRaw =
   327:         contextualCounts.get(prefixo) || 0;
   328: 
   329:       const recentRaw =
   330:         recentCounts.get(prefixo) || 0;
   331: 
   332:       const fatigueRaw =
   333:         fatigueCounts.get(prefixo) || 0;
   334: 
   335:       const lastSeenRaw =
   336:         lastSeen.get(prefixo) || 0;
   337: 
   338:       const distributionRaw =
   339:         distribution.get(prefixo) || 0;
   340: 
   341:       const contextualFrequency = normalizeRatio(
   342:         contextualRaw,
   343:         maxContextual
   344:       );
   345: 
   346:       const recentTrend = normalizeRatio(
   347:         recentRaw,
   348:         maxRecent
   349:       );
   350: 
   351:       const chronologicalRecency = normalizeRatio(
   352:         lastSeenRaw,
   353:         maxLastSeen
   354:       );
   355: 
   356:       const temporalDistribution = normalizeRatio(
   357:         distributionRaw,
   358:         maxDistribution
   359:       );
   360: 
   361:       /*
   362:        * Fadiga:
   363:        * somente unidades acima da média recente recebem penalidade.
   364:        */
   365:       const expectedFatigue =
   366:         fatigueRows.length / 10;
   367: 
   368:       const fatigueExcess =
   369:         expectedFatigue > 0
   370:           ? Math.max(
   371:               0,
   372:               (fatigueRaw - expectedFatigue) /
   373:                 expectedFatigue
   374:             )
   375:           : 0;
   376: 
   377:       const fatigueNormalized =
   378:         maxFatigue > 0
   379:           ? Math.min(
   380:               1,
   381:               fatigueExcess /
   382:                 Math.max(1, maxFatigue)
   383:             )
   384:           : 0;
   385: 
   386:       const baseScore =
   387:         contextualFrequency *
   388:           normalizedWeights.contextualFrequency +
   389:         recentTrend *
   390:           normalizedWeights.recentTrend +
   391:         chronologicalRecency *
   392:           normalizedWeights.chronologicalRecency +
   393:         temporalDistribution *
   394:           normalizedWeights.temporalDistribution;
   395: 
   396:       const penalty =
   397:         fatigueNormalized *
   398:         Math.max(
   399:           0,
   400:           safeNumber(
   401:             fatiguePenalty,
   402:             DEFAULT_FATIGUE_PENALTY
   403:           )
   404:         );
   405: 
   406:       const score = Math.max(0, baseScore - penalty);
   407: 
   408:       return {
   409:         prefixo,
   410:         score: Number((score * 100).toFixed(4)),
   411:         evidence: {
   412:           contextualFrequency: {
   413:             count: contextualRaw,
   414:             normalized: Number(
   415:               contextualFrequency.toFixed(6)
   416:             ),
   417:           },
   418:           recentTrend: {
   419:             count: recentRaw,
   420:             window: recentSize,
   421:             normalized: Number(
   422:               recentTrend.toFixed(6)
   423:             ),
   424:           },
   425:           chronologicalRecency: {
   426:             lastSeenSequence: lastSeenRaw,
   427:             maxSequence: maxLastSeen,
   428:             normalized: Number(
   429:               chronologicalRecency.toFixed(6)
   430:             ),
   431:           },
   432:           temporalDistribution: {
   433:             distinctDates: distributionRaw,
   434:             normalized: Number(
   435:               temporalDistribution.toFixed(6)
   436:             ),
   437:           },
   438:           fatigue: {
   439:             count: fatigueRaw,
   440:             window: fatigueSize,
   441:             expected: Number(
   442:               expectedFatigue.toFixed(6)
   443:             ),
   444:             normalized: Number(
   445:               fatigueNormalized.toFixed(6)
   446:             ),
   447:             penalty: Number(
   448:               (penalty * 100).toFixed(4)
   449:             ),
   450:           },
   451:         },
   452:       };
   453:     }
   454:   );
   455: 
   456:   ranking.sort((a, b) => {
   457:     if (b.score !== a.score) {
   458:       return b.score - a.score;
   459:     }
   460: 
   461:     const bContext =
   462:       b.evidence.contextualFrequency.count;
   463:     const aContext =
   464:       a.evidence.contextualFrequency.count;
   465: 
   466:     if (bContext !== aContext) {
   467:       return bContext - aContext;
   468:     }
   469: 
   470:     const bRecent =
   471:       b.evidence.recentTrend.count;
   472:     const aRecent =
   473:       a.evidence.recentTrend.count;
   474: 
   475:     if (bRecent !== aRecent) {
   476:       return bRecent - aRecent;
   477:     }
   478: 
   479:     return Number(a.prefixo) - Number(b.prefixo);
   480:   });
   481: 
   482:   return ranking.map((item, index) => ({
   483:     position: index + 1,
   484:     ...item,
   485:   }));
   486: }
   487: 
   488: export function buildMilharRecommendationV3({
   489:   centena,
   490:   prizes = [],
   491:   fallbackPrizes = [],
   492:   ...options
   493: } = {}) {
   494:   const centena3 = normalizeCentena3V3(centena);
   495: 
   496:   if (!centena3) {
   497:     return {
   498:       ok: false,
   499:       status: "invalid_centena",
   500:       model: "MILHAR_CONTEXTUAL_V3",
   501:       centena: "",
   502:       milhar: null,
   503:       prefixo: null,
   504:       score: 0,
   505:       confidence: 0,
   506:       evidence: null,
   507:       alternatives: [],
   508:       candidates: [],
   509:     };
   510:   }
   511: 
   512:   const prefixRanking = buildPrefixRankingV3({
   513:     prizes,
   514:     fallbackPrizes,
   515:     ...options,
   516:   });
   517: 
   518:   if (!prefixRanking.length) {
   519:     return {
   520:       ok: false,
   521:       status: "insufficient_evidence",
   522:       model: "MILHAR_CONTEXTUAL_V3",
   523:       centena: centena3,
   524:       milhar: null,
   525:       prefixo: null,
   526:       score: 0,
   527:       confidence: 0,
   528:       evidence: null,
   529:       alternatives: [],
   530:       candidates: [],
   531:     };
   532:   }
   533: 
   534:   const candidates = prefixRanking.map((item) => ({
   535:     position: item.position,
   536:     prefixo: item.prefixo,
   537:     milhar: `${item.prefixo}${centena3}`,
   538:     score: item.score,
   539:     evidence: item.evidence,
   540:   }));
   541: 
   542:   const winner = candidates[0];
   543: 
   544:   return {
   545:     ok: true,
   546:     status: "recommended",
   547:     model: "MILHAR_CONTEXTUAL_V3",
   548:     centena: centena3,
   549:     milhar: winner.milhar,
   550:     prefixo: winner.prefixo,
   551:     score: winner.score,
   552:     confidence: Number(
   553:       (winner.score / 100).toFixed(6)
   554:     ),
   555:     evidence: winner.evidence,
   556:     alternatives: candidates.slice(0, 3),
   557:     candidates,
   558:   };
   559: }
   560: 
   561: export function diversifyMilharRecommendationsV3(
   562:   rows = [],
   563:   {
   564:     maxPerPrefix = 4,
   565:     repeatPenalty = 12,
   566:   } = {}
   567: ) {
   568:   const source = Array.isArray(rows) ? rows : [];
   569:   const usage = new Map();
   570: 
   571:   return source.map((row) => {
   572:     const centena =
   573:       normalizeCentena3V3(row?.centena);
   574: 
   575:     const candidates = Array.isArray(
   576:       row?.recommendation?.candidates
   577:     )
   578:       ? row.recommendation.candidates
   579:           .filter((candidate) => {
   580:             const milhar =
   581:               normalizeMilhar4V3(candidate?.milhar);
   582: 
   583:             return (
   584:               milhar &&
   585:               milhar.slice(-3) === centena
   586:             );
   587:           })
   588:           .map((candidate) => ({
   589:             ...candidate,
   590:             milhar: normalizeMilhar4V3(
   591:               candidate.milhar
   592:             ),
   593:             prefixo: String(
   594:               candidate?.prefixo ??
   595:                 normalizeMilhar4V3(
   596:                   candidate.milhar
   597:                 ).slice(0, 1)
   598:             ),
   599:             score: safeNumber(
   600:               candidate?.score,
   601:               0
   602:             ),
   603:           }))
   604:       : [];
   605: 
   606:     if (!candidates.length) {
   607:       return row;
   608:     }
   609: 
   610:     const ranked = candidates
   611:       .map((candidate) => {
   612:         const used =
   613:           usage.get(candidate.prefixo) || 0;
   614: 
   615:         const overLimitPenalty =
   616:           used >= Number(maxPerPrefix || 4)
   617:             ? Number(repeatPenalty || 0) * 2
   618:             : 0;
   619: 
   620:         const adjustedScore =
   621:           candidate.score -
   622:           used * Number(repeatPenalty || 0) -
   623:           overLimitPenalty;
   624: 
   625:         return {
   626:           ...candidate,
   627:           used,
   628:           adjustedScore,
   629:         };
   630:       })
   631:       .sort((a, b) => {
   632:         if (
   633:           b.adjustedScore !==
   634:           a.adjustedScore
   635:         ) {
   636:           return (
   637:             b.adjustedScore -
   638:             a.adjustedScore
   639:           );
   640:         }
   641: 
   642:         if (b.score !== a.score) {
   643:           return b.score - a.score;
   644:         }
   645: 
   646:         return (
   647:           Number(a.prefixo) -
   648:           Number(b.prefixo)
   649:         );
   650:       });
   651: 
   652:     const selected = ranked[0];
   653: 
   654:     usage.set(
   655:       selected.prefixo,
   656:       (usage.get(selected.prefixo) || 0) + 1
   657:     );
   658: 
   659:     return {
   660:       ...row,
   661:       milhar: selected.milhar,
   662:       recommendation: {
   663:         ...(row?.recommendation || {}),
   664:         milhar: selected.milhar,
   665:         prefixo: selected.prefixo,
   666:         diversified: true,
   667:         originalMilhar:
   668:           row?.recommendation?.milhar || null,
   669:         diversity: {
   670:           prefixUsageBefore: selected.used,
   671:           adjustedScore: Number(
   672:             selected.adjustedScore.toFixed(4)
   673:           ),
   674:           maxPerPrefix: Number(
   675:             maxPerPrefix || 4
   676:           ),
   677:           repeatPenalty: Number(
   678:             repeatPenalty || 0
   679:           ),
   680:         },
   681:       },
   682:     };
   683:   });

  3602: function getDezenasFixasFromGrupo(grupo2) {
  3603:   const g = Number(grupo2);
  3604:   if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > 25) return [];
  3605: 
  3606:   const start = (g - 1) * 4 + 1;
  3607:   const out = [];
  3608: 
  3609:   for (let i = 0; i < 4; i += 1) {
  3610:     out.push(wrapToDezena2(start + i));
  3611:   }
  3612: 
  3613:   return out.filter(Boolean);
  3614: }

  3617: export function buildMilharesForGrupo({
  3618:   rangeDraws,
  3619:   analysisHourBucket,
  3620:   schedule,
  3621:   grupo2,
  3622:   count = 20,
  3623:   targetYmd = "",
  3624: }) {
  3625:   const list = Array.isArray(rangeDraws) ? rangeDraws : [];
  3626:   const target = toHourBucket(analysisHourBucket);
  3627:   const schSet = scheduleSet(schedule);
  3628:   const targetDay = safeStr(targetYmd);
  3629:   const targetDow = isYMD(targetDay) ? getDowKey(targetDay) : null;
  3630: 
  3631:   const N = Number.isFinite(Number(count))
  3632:     ? Math.max(4, Math.trunc(Number(count)))
  3633:     : 20;
  3634: 
  3635:   const grupoNum = Number(grupo2);
  3636: 
  3637:   if (!Number.isFinite(grupoNum) || grupoNum < 1 || grupoNum > 25 || !list.length) {
  3638:     return { dezenas: [], slots: [] };
  3639:   }
  3640: 
  3641:   const dezenasFixas = getDezenasFixasFromGrupo(grupoNum);
  3642:   if (!dezenasFixas.length) return { dezenas: [], slots: [] };
  3643: 
  3644:   const perDezena = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(N / dezenasFixas.length));
  3645:   const byMilhar = new Map();
  3646: 
  3647:   for (const d of list) {
  3648:     const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
  3649:     const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
  3650:     if (!h) continue;
  3651: 
  3652:     const isTargetHour = target && h === target;
  3653:     const isScheduleHour = schSet.size && schSet.has(h);
  3654:     const isSameDow = targetDow !== null && Number(getDowKey(y)) === Number(targetDow);
  3655:     const ts = ymdHourToTs(y, h);
  3656: 
  3657:     const ps = Array.isArray(d?.prizes) ? d.prizes : [];
  3658: 
  3659:     for (const prize of ps) {
  3660:       const pos = Number(guessPrizePos(prize));
  3661:       if (!Number.isFinite(pos) || pos < 1 || pos > 7) continue;
  3662: 
  3663:       const g = Number(guessPrizeGrupo(prize));
  3664:       if (g !== grupoNum) continue;
  3665: 
  3666:       const m4 = String(pickPrizeMilhar4(prize) || "").replace(/\D+/g, "").padStart(4, "0").slice(-4);
  3667:       if (!/^\d{4}$/.test(m4)) continue;
  3668: 
  3669:       const dz = getDezena2(m4);
  3670:       const c3 = getCentena3(m4);
  3671: 
  3672:       if (!dz || !c3 || !dezenasFixas.includes(dz)) continue;
  3673: 
  3674:       const current = byMilhar.get(m4) || {
  3675:         milhar: m4,
  3676:         dezena: dz,
  3677:         centena: c3,
  3678:         freq: 0,
  3679:         score: 0,
  3680:         targetHits: 0,
  3681:         scheduleHits: 0,
  3682:         sameDowHits: 0,
  3683:         firstPrizeHits: 0,
  3684:         lastTs: 0,
  3685:       };
  3686: 
  3687:       let inc = 1;
  3688: 
  3689:       if (isTargetHour) {
  3690:         inc += 8;
  3691:         current.targetHits += 1;
  3692:       }
  3693: 
  3694:       if (isScheduleHour) {
  3695:         inc += 3;
  3696:         current.scheduleHits += 1;
  3697:       }
  3698: 
  3699:       if (isSameDow) {
  3700:         inc += 2;
  3701:         current.sameDowHits += 1;
  3702:       }
  3703: 
  3704:       if (pos === 1) {
  3705:         inc += 3;
  3706:         current.firstPrizeHits += 1;
  3707:       } else if (pos <= 3) {
  3708:         inc += 1;
  3709:       }
  3710: 
  3711:       current.freq += 1;
  3712:       current.score += inc;
  3713: 
  3714:       if (Number.isFinite(ts)) {
  3715:         current.lastTs = Math.max(Number(current.lastTs || 0), ts);
  3716:       }
  3717: 
  3718:       byMilhar.set(m4, current);
  3719:     }
  3720:   }
  3721: 
  3722:   const ranked = Array.from(byMilhar.values())
  3723:     .map((m) => {
  3724:       const freq = Number(m.freq || 0);
  3725:       const targetHits = Number(m.targetHits || 0);
  3726:       const scheduleHits = Number(m.scheduleHits || 0);
  3727:       const sameDowHits = Number(m.sameDowHits || 0);
  3728:       const firstPrizeHits = Number(m.firstPrizeHits || 0);
  3729:       const recencyBoost = Number(m.lastTs || 0) > 0 ? 1 : 0;
  3730: 
  3731:       return {
  3732:         ...m,
  3733:         score:
  3734:           freq * 100 +
  3735:           targetHits * 70 +
  3736:           scheduleHits * 35 +
  3737:           sameDowHits * 25 +
  3738:           firstPrizeHits * 45 +
  3739:           recencyBoost * 5,
  3740:       };
  3741:     })
  3742:     .sort((a, b) => {
  3743:       if (Number(b.score) !== Number(a.score)) return Number(b.score) - Number(a.score);
  3744:       if (Number(b.targetHits) !== Number(a.targetHits)) return Number(b.targetHits) - Number(a.targetHits);
  3745:       if (Number(b.freq) !== Number(a.freq)) return Number(b.freq) - Number(a.freq);
  3746:       if (Number(b.lastTs) !== Number(a.lastTs)) return Number(b.lastTs) - Number(a.lastTs);
  3747:       return milharCompareAsc(a.milhar, b.milhar);
  3748:     });
  3749: 
  3750:   const centenaStats = new Map();
  3751: 
  3752:   for (const item of ranked) {
  3753:     const centena = String(item?.centena || "").trim();
  3754:     if (!centena) continue;
  3755: 
  3756:     const current = centenaStats.get(centena) || {
  3757:       centena,

  3761:       sameDowHits: 0,
  3762:       firstPrizeHits: 0,
  3763:       lastTs: 0,
  3764:     };
  3765: 
  3766:     current.freq += Number(item.freq || 0);
  3767:     current.score += Number(item.score || 0);
  3768:     current.targetHits += Number(item.targetHits || 0);
  3769:     current.sameDowHits += Number(item.sameDowHits || 0);
  3770:     current.firstPrizeHits += Number(item.firstPrizeHits || 0);
  3771:     current.lastTs = Math.max(Number(current.lastTs || 0), Number(item.lastTs || 0));
  3772: 
  3773:     centenaStats.set(centena, current);
  3774:   }
  3775: 
  3776:   const centenaScoreMap = new Map();
  3777: 
  3778:   for (const c of centenaStats.values()) {
  3779:     centenaScoreMap.set(
  3780:       c.centena,
  3781:       Number(c.score || 0) +
  3782:         Number(c.freq || 0) * 25 +
  3783:         Number(c.targetHits || 0) * 60 +
  3784:         Number(c.sameDowHits || 0) * 30 +
  3785:         Number(c.firstPrizeHits || 0) * 40 +
  3786:         (Number(c.lastTs || 0) > 0 ? 10 : 0)
  3787:     );
  3788:   }
  3789: 
  3790:   /*
  3791:    * Contexto compartilhado do seletor de milhar.
  3792:    *
  3793:    * O Top3 continua escolhendo:
  3794:    * - grupo;
  3795:    * - dezenas;
  3796:    * - centenas.
  3797:    *
  3798:    * O chooseBestMilhar escolhe somente o prefixo vencedor
  3799:    * para cada centena selecionada.
  3800:    */
  3801:   const fallbackMilharPrizes = [];
  3802:   const primaryMilharPrizes = [];
  3803: 
  3804:   for (const draw of list) {
  3805:     const drawYmd = pickDrawYMD(draw);
  3806:     const drawHour = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(draw));
  3807:     const drawDow =
  3808:       isYMD(drawYmd)
  3809:         ? getDowKey(drawYmd)
  3810:         : null;
  3811: 
  3812:     const matchesTargetHour =
  3813:       Boolean(target) &&
  3814:       drawHour === target;
  3815: 
  3816:     const matchesTargetDow =
  3817:       targetDow === null ||
  3818:       Number(drawDow) === Number(targetDow);
  3819: 
  3820:     const prizes = Array.isArray(draw?.prizes)
  3821:       ? draw.prizes
  3822:       : [];
  3823: 
  3824:     for (const prize of prizes) {
  3825:       const position = Number(guessPrizePos(prize));
  3826:       const prizeGroup = Number(guessPrizeGrupo(prize));
  3827: 
  3828:       if (
  3829:         !Number.isFinite(position) ||
  3830:         position < 1 ||
  3831:         position > 7 ||
  3832:         prizeGroup !== grupoNum
  3833:       ) {
  3834:         continue;
  3835:       }
  3836: 
  3837:       fallbackMilharPrizes.push(prize);
  3838: 
  3839:       if (matchesTargetHour && matchesTargetDow) {
  3840:         primaryMilharPrizes.push(prize);
  3841:       }
  3842:     }
  3843:   }
  3844: 
  3845:   const usedMilhares = new Set();
  3846:   const slots = [];
  3847: 
  3848:   function pickDiversifiedMilharesForDezena(dz, limit) {
  3849:     const candidates = ranked
  3850:       .filter((item) => item.dezena === dz)
  3851:       .map((item) => {
  3852:         const centena = String(
  3853:           item?.centena || ""
  3854:         )
  3855:           .replace(/\D+/g, "")
  3856:           .padStart(3, "0")
  3857:           .slice(-3);
  3858: 
  3859:         return {
  3860:           ...item,
  3861:           centena,
  3862:           centenaScore: Number(
  3863:             centenaScoreMap.get(centena) || 0
  3864:           ),
  3865:         };
  3866:       })
  3867:       .filter((item) => /^\d{3}$/.test(item.centena));
  3868: 
  3869:     const byCentena = new Map();
  3870: 
  3871:     for (const item of candidates) {
  3872:       const current = byCentena.get(item.centena);
  3873: 
  3874:       if (
  3875:         !current ||
  3876:         Number(item.centenaScore || 0) >
  3877:           Number(current.centenaScore || 0) ||
  3878:         (
  3879:           Number(item.centenaScore || 0) ===
  3880:             Number(current.centenaScore || 0) &&
  3881:           Number(item.score || 0) >
  3882:             Number(current.score || 0)
  3883:         )
  3884:       ) {
  3885:         byCentena.set(item.centena, item);
  3886:       }
  3887:     }
  3888: 
  3889:     const strongestCentenas = Array.from(
  3890:       byCentena.values()
  3891:     ).sort((a, b) => {
  3892:       if (
  3893:         Number(b.centenaScore || 0) !==
  3894:         Number(a.centenaScore || 0)
  3895:       ) {
  3896:         return (
  3897:           Number(b.centenaScore || 0) -
  3898:           Number(a.centenaScore || 0)
  3899:         );
  3900:       }
  3901: 
  3902:       if (
  3903:         Number(b.score || 0) !==
  3904:         Number(a.score || 0)
  3905:       ) {
  3906:         return (
  3907:           Number(b.score || 0) -
  3908:           Number(a.score || 0)
  3909:         );
  3910:       }
  3911: 
  3912:       if (
  3913:         Number(b.targetHits || 0) !==
  3914:         Number(a.targetHits || 0)
  3915:       ) {
  3916:         return (
  3917:           Number(b.targetHits || 0) -
  3918:           Number(a.targetHits || 0)
  3919:         );
  3920:       }
  3921: 
  3922:       if (
  3923:         Number(b.freq || 0) !==

  3952:         sharedWinner?.milhar || ""
  3953:       )
  3954:         .replace(/\D+/g, "")
  3955:         .padStart(4, "0")
  3956:         .slice(-4);

  3959:         continue;
  3960:       }

  3964:         centenaItem.centena
  3965:       ) {
  3966:         continue;
  3967:       }
  3968: 
  3969:       if (
  3970:         getDezena2(selectedMilhar) !== dz ||
  3971:         usedMilhares.has(selectedMilhar) ||
  3972:         picked.some(
  3973:           (item) =>
  3974:             item.milhar === selectedMilhar
  3975:         )
  3976:       ) {
  3977:         continue;
  3978:       }
  3979: 
  3980:       picked.push({
  3981:         ...centenaItem,
  3982:         milhar: selectedMilhar,
  3983:         prefix:
  3984:           sharedWinner?.prefix ??
  3985:           selectedMilhar.slice(0, 1),
  3986:         adjustedScore: Number(
  3987:           sharedWinner?.score ||
  3988:           centenaItem.score ||
  3989:           0
  3990:         ),
  3991:         sharedMilharModel:
  3992:           sharedResult?.model ||
  3993:           "MILHAR_PROBABILITY_V2",
  3994:         sharedMilharSampleSize: Number(
  3995:           sharedResult?.sampleSize || 0
  3996:         ),
  3997:       });
  3998:     }
  3999: 
  4000:     return picked.slice(0, limit);
  4001:   }
  4002: 
  4003:   for (const dz of dezenasFixas) {
  4004:     const items = pickDiversifiedMilharesForDezena(dz, perDezena);
  4005: 
  4006:     let pushed = 0;
  4007: 
  4008:     for (const item of items) {
  4009:       usedMilhares.add(item.milhar);
  4010:       slots.push({
  4011:         dezena: dz,
  4012:         milhar: item.milhar,
  4013:         score: item.score,
  4014:         adjustedScore: item.adjustedScore,
  4015:         centena: item.centena,
  4016:         centenaScore: item.centenaScore,
  4017:         freq: item.freq,
  4018:         targetHits: item.targetHits,
  4019:         scheduleHits: item.scheduleHits,
  4020:         sameDowHits: item.sameDowHits,
  4021:         firstPrizeHits: item.firstPrizeHits,
  4022:       });
  4023:       pushed += 1;
  4024:     }
  4025: 
  4026:     while (pushed < perDezena) {
  4027:       slots.push({ dezena: dz, milhar: "" });
  4028:       pushed += 1;
  4029:     }
  4030:   }
  4031: 
  4032:   while (slots.length < N) slots.push({ dezena: "", milhar: "" });
  4033: 
  4034:   return { dezenas: dezenasFixas, slots: slots.slice(0, N) };
  4035: }
  4036: 
  4037: export function build16MilharesForGrupo(args) {
  4038:   return buildMilharesForGrupo({ ...(args || {}), count: 16 });
  4039: }

  4051: export function auditTop3Timeline({
  4052:   timeline,
  4053:   lotteryKey = "",
  4054: }) {
  4055:   const rows = Array.isArray(timeline) ? timeline : [];
  4056: 
  4057:   const validated = rows.filter((slot) => {
  4058:     const status = String(slot?.status || "").toLowerCase();
  4059: 
  4060:     return (
  4061:       status === "validated" &&
  4062:       Number.isFinite(Number(slot?.resultGrupo))
  4063:     );
  4064:   });
  4065: 
  4066:   function pct(n, d) {
  4067:     if (!d) return 0;
  4068: 
  4069:     return Number(
  4070:       ((Number(n || 0) / Number(d || 1)) * 100).toFixed(2)
  4071:     );
  4072:   }
  4073: 
  4074:   function normalizeResultTop3(slot) {
  4075:     const source = Array.isArray(slot?.resultTop3Groups)
  4076:       ? slot.resultTop3Groups
  4077:       : [];
  4078: 
  4079:     const normalized = source
  4080:       .slice(0, 3)
  4081:       .map((grupo) => {
  4082:         const value = Number(grupo);
  4083: 
  4084:         return Number.isFinite(value) &&
  4085:           value >= 1 &&
  4086:           value <= TOP3_GROUPS_K
  4087:           ? value
  4088:           : null;
  4089:       });
  4090: 
  4091:     while (normalized.length < 3) {
  4092:       normalized.push(null);
  4093:     }
  4094: 
  4095:     if (!Number.isFinite(Number(normalized[0]))) {
  4096:       const fallback = Number(slot?.resultGrupo);
  4097: 
  4098:       normalized[0] =
  4099:         Number.isFinite(fallback) &&
  4100:         fallback >= 1 &&
  4101:         fallback <= TOP3_GROUPS_K
  4102:           ? fallback

  4066:   function pct(n, d) {
  4067:     if (!d) return 0;
  4068: 
  4069:     return Number(
  4070:       ((Number(n || 0) / Number(d || 1)) * 100).toFixed(2)
  4071:     );
  4072:   }
  4073: 
  4074:   function normalizeResultTop3(slot) {
  4075:     const source = Array.isArray(slot?.resultTop3Groups)
  4076:       ? slot.resultTop3Groups
  4077:       : [];

  4084:         return Number.isFinite(value) &&

  4087:           ? value
  4088:           : null;
  4089:       });

  4092:       normalized.push(null);

  4096:       const fallback = Number(slot?.resultGrupo);

    36: function findPreviousValidDraw(draws, currentYmd, currentHour) {
    37:   const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
    38:   const targetY = safeStr(currentYmd);
    39:   const targetH = toHourBucket(currentHour);
    40: 
    41:   if (!isYMD(targetY) || !targetH) return null;
    42: 
    43:   const targetTs = ymdHourToTs(targetY, targetH);
    44: 
    45:   return list
    46:     .filter((draw) => Array.isArray(draw?.prizes) && draw.prizes.length > 0)
    47:     .map((draw) => {
    48:       const y = pickDrawYMD(draw);
    49:       const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(draw));
    50:       return { draw, ts: ymdHourToTs(y, h) };
    51:     })
    52:     .filter((x) => Number.isFinite(x.ts) && x.ts < targetTs)
    53:     .sort((a, b) => b.ts - a.ts)[0]?.draw || null;
    54: }
    55: 
    56: /* =========================
    57:    Draw helpers (robustos)
    58: ========================= */
    59: 
    60: function grupoFromDezena2(dezena2) {
    61:   const s = safeStr(dezena2);
    62:   if (!/^\d{2}$/.test(s)) return null;
    63: 
    64:   const n = Number(s);
    65:   if (!Number.isFinite(n) || n < 0 || n > 99) return null;
    66:   if (n === 0) return 25;
    67: 
    68:   const g = Math.ceil(n / 4);
    69:   return g >= 1 && g <= 25 ? g : null;
    70: }
    71: 
    72: function drawQualityScore(draw) {
    73:   const prizes = Array.isArray(draw?.prizes) ? draw.prizes : [];
    74:   const y = pickDrawYMD(draw);
    75:   const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(draw));
    76: 
    77:   let score = 0;
    78:   if (isYMD(y)) score += 20;
    79:   if (h) score += 20;
    80:   score += Math.min(10, prizes.length) * 10;
    81: 
    82:   const hasP1 = prizes.some((p) => guessPrizePos(p) === 1);
    83:   if (hasP1) score += 15;
    84: 
    85:   return score;
    86: }
    87: 
    88: export function guessPrizePos(p) {
    89:   const pos = Number.isFinite(Number(p?.position))
    90:     ? Number(p.position)
    91:     : Number.isFinite(Number(p?.posicao))
    92:       ? Number(p.posicao)
    93:       : Number.isFinite(Number(p?.pos))
    94:         ? Number(p.pos)
    95:         : Number.isFinite(Number(p?.colocacao))
    96:           ? Number(p.colocacao)
    97:           : null;
    98: 
    99:   return pos;
   100: }
   101: 
   102: export function guessPrizeGrupo(p) {
   103:   const directCandidates = [
   104:     p?.grupo2,
   105:     p?.group2,
   106:     p?.grupo,
   107:     p?.group,
   108:     p?.animal_grupo,
   109:     p?.grupo_animal,
   110:     p?.grupoAnimal,
   111:     p?.g,
   112:   ];
   113: 
   114:   for (const v of directCandidates) {
   115:     const g = Number(v);
   116:     if (Number.isFinite(g) && g >= 1 && g <= 25) {
   117:       return g;
   118:     }
   119:   }
   120: 
   121:   const milhar4 = pickPrizeMilhar4(p);
   122: 
   123:   if (milhar4) {
   124:     const dezena2 = getDezena2(milhar4);
   125:     const derived = grupoFromDezena2(dezena2);
   126: 
   127:     if (Number.isFinite(derived)) return derived;
   128:   }
   129: 
   130:   return null;
   131: }
   132: 
   133: export function pickDrawHour(draw) {
   134:   return normalizeHourLike(
   135:     draw?.close_hour || draw?.closeHour || draw?.hour || draw?.hora || ""
   136:   );
   137: }
   138: 
   139: export function pickDrawYMD(draw) {
   140:   const y =
   141:     draw?.ymd ||
   142:     normalizeToYMD(draw?.date) ||
   143:     normalizeToYMD(draw?.data) ||
   144:     normalizeToYMD(draw?.dt) ||
   145:     normalizeToYMD(draw?.draw_date) ||
   146:     normalizeToYMD(draw?.drawDate) ||
   147:     normalizeToYMD(draw?.close_date) ||
   148:     normalizeToYMD(draw?.closeDate) ||
   149:     null;
   150: 
   151:   return y;
   152: }
   153: 
   154: export function pickPrize1GrupoFromDraw(draw) {
   155:   const prizes = Array.isArray(draw?.prizes) ? draw.prizes : [];
   156:   if (prizes.length) {
   157:     const p1 = prizes.find((p) => guessPrizePos(p) === 1) || null;
   158:     if (p1) {
   159:       const g = guessPrizeGrupo(p1);
   160:       return Number.isFinite(Number(g)) ? Number(g) : null;

  3840:         primaryMilharPrizes.push(prize);

  3600: function getDezenasFixasFromGrupo(grupo2) {
  3601:   const g = Number(grupo2);
  3602:   if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > 25) return [];
  3603: 
  3604:   const start = (g - 1) * 4 + 1;

  3615: export function buildMilharesForGrupo({
  3616:   rangeDraws,
  3617:   analysisHourBucket,
  3618:   schedule,
  3619:   grupo2,
  3620:   count = 20,
  3621:   targetYmd = "",
  3622: }) {

  3630:     ? Math.max(4, Math.trunc(Number(count)))

  3634: 
  3635:   if (!Number.isFinite(grupoNum) || grupoNum < 1 || grupoNum > 25 || !list.length) {
  3636:     return { dezenas: [], slots: [] };
  3637:   }
  3638: 
  3639:   const dezenasFixas = getDezenasFixasFromGrupo(grupoNum);

  3641: 
  3642:   const perDezena = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(N / dezenasFixas.length));
  3643:   const byMilhar = new Map();
  3644: 
  3645:   for (const d of list) {
  3646:     const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
  3647:     const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));

  3649: 
  3650:     const isTargetHour = target && h === target;
  3651:     const isScheduleHour = schSet.size && schSet.has(h);
  3652:     const isSameDow = targetDow !== null && Number(getDowKey(y)) === Number(targetDow);
  3653:     const ts = ymdHourToTs(y, h);
  3654: 
  3655:     const ps = Array.isArray(d?.prizes) ? d.prizes : [];

  3662:       if (g !== grupoNum) continue;

  3686:

  3688:         inc += 8;

  3690:       }
  3691:

  3693:         inc += 3;

  3698:         inc += 2;

  3700:       }
  3701:

  3703:         inc += 3;
  3704:         current.firstPrizeHits += 1;
  3705:       } else if (pos <= 3) {
  3706:         inc += 1;

  3710:       current.score += inc;

  3822:         const sortedItems = items.slice().sort((a, b) => {
  3823:           if (Number(b.adjustedScore) !== Number(a.adjustedScore)) {
  3824:             return Number(b.adjustedScore) - Number(a.adjustedScore);
  3825:           }
  3826:           if (Number(b.score) !== Number(a.score)) return Number(b.score) - Number(a.score);
  3827:           if (Number(b.targetHits) !== Number(a.targetHits)) return Number(b.targetHits) - Number(a.targetHits);
  3828:           if (Number(b.freq) !== Number(a.freq)) return Number(b.freq) - Number(a.freq);
  3829:           return milharCompareAsc(a.milhar, b.milhar);
  3830:         });
  3831: 
  3832:         const top = sortedItems[0] || {};
  3833: 
  3834:         return {
  3835:           centena,
  3836:           centenaScore: Number(top.centenaScore || 0),
  3837:           bestScore: Number(top.adjustedScore || 0),
  3838:           items: sortedItems,
  3839:         };
  3840:       })
  3841:       .sort((a, b) => {
  3842:         if (Number(b.centenaScore) !== Number(a.centenaScore)) {
  3843:           return Number(b.centenaScore) - Number(a.centenaScore);
  3844:         }
  3845:         if (Number(b.bestScore) !== Number(a.bestScore)) {
  3846:           return Number(b.bestScore) - Number(a.bestScore);
  3847:         }
  3848:         return String(a.centena).localeCompare(String(b.centena));
  3849:       });
  3850: 
  3851:     const picked = [];
  3852:     const usedPrefixes = new Set();
  3853:     const usedCentenas = new Set();
  3854: 
  3855:     // Fase 1: uma melhor milhar por centena forte, evitando repetir prefixo.
  3856:     for (const group of centenaGroups) {
  3857:       if (picked.length >= limit) break;
  3858: 
  3859:       const item = group.items.find((x) => !usedPrefixes.has(x.prefix));
  3860:       if (!item) continue;
  3861: 
  3862:       picked.push(item);
  3863:       usedPrefixes.add(item.prefix);
  3864:       usedCentenas.add(item.centena);
  3865:     }
  3866: 
  3867:     // Fase 2: se faltar, pega novas centenas fortes mesmo repetindo prefixo.
  3868:     for (const group of centenaGroups) {
  3869:       if (picked.length >= limit) break;
  3870:       if (usedCentenas.has(group.centena)) continue;
  3871: 
  3872:       const item = group.items.find((x) => !picked.some((p) => p.milhar === x.milhar));
  3873:       if (!item) continue;
  3874: 
  3875:       picked.push(item);
  3876:       usedPrefixes.add(item.prefix);
  3877:       usedCentenas.add(item.centena);
  3878:     }
  3879: 
  3880:     // Fase 3: se ainda faltar, permite mais de uma milhar da mesma centena.
  3881:     for (const group of centenaGroups) {
  3882:       if (picked.length >= limit) break;
  3883: 
  3884:       for (const item of group.items) {
  3885:         if (picked.length >= limit) break;
  3886:         if (picked.some((p) => p.milhar === item.milhar)) continue;
  3887: 
  3888:         picked.push(item);
  3889:       }
  3890:     }
  3891: 
  3892:     return picked.slice(0, limit);
  3893:   }
  3894: 
  3895:   for (const dz of dezenasFixas) {
  3896:     const items = pickDiversifiedMilharesForDezena(dz, perDezena);
  3897: 
  3898:     let pushed = 0;
  3899: 
  3900:     for (const item of items) {
  3901:       usedMilhares.add(item.milhar);
  3902:       slots.push({
  3903:         dezena: dz,
  3904:         milhar: item.milhar,
  3905:         score: item.score,
  3906:         adjustedScore: item.adjustedScore,
  3907:         centena: item.centena,
  3908:         centenaScore: item.centenaScore,
  3909:         freq: item.freq,
  3910:         targetHits: item.targetHits,
  3911:         scheduleHits: item.scheduleHits,
  3912:         sameDowHits: item.sameDowHits,
  3913:         firstPrizeHits: item.firstPrizeHits,
  3914:       });
  3915:       pushed += 1;
  3916:     }
  3917: 
  3918:     while (pushed < perDezena) {
  3919:       slots.push({ dezena: dz, milhar: "" });
  3920:       pushed += 1;
  3921:     }
  3922:   }
  3923: 
  3924:   while (slots.length < N) slots.push({ dezena: "", milhar: "" });
  3925: 
  3926:   return { dezenas: dezenasFixas, slots: slots.slice(0, N) };
  3927: }
  3928: 
  3929: export function build16MilharesForGrupo(args) {
  3930:   return buildMilharesForGrupo({ ...(args || {}), count: 16 });
  3931: }
  3932: 
  3933: export function build20MilharesForGrupo(args) {
  3934:   return buildMilharesForGrupo({ ...(args || {}), count: 20 });
  3935: }

  3970:

  3763: 
  3764:     current.freq += Number(item.freq || 0);
  3765:     current.score += Number(item.score || 0);
  3766:     current.targetHits += Number(item.targetHits || 0);
  3767:     current.sameDowHits += Number(item.sameDowHits || 0);
  3768:     current.firstPrizeHits += Number(item.firstPrizeHits || 0);
  3769:     current.lastTs = Math.max(Number(current.lastTs || 0), Number(item.lastTs || 0));
  3770: 
  3771:     centenaStats.set(centena, current);
  3772:   }

  3774:   const centenaScoreMap = new Map();
  3775: 
  3776:   for (const c of centenaStats.values()) {
  3777:     centenaScoreMap.set(
  3778:       c.centena,
  3779:       Number(c.score || 0) +
  3780:         Number(c.freq || 0) * 25 +
  3781:         Number(c.targetHits || 0) * 60 +
  3782:         Number(c.sameDowHits || 0) * 30 +

  3788:   const usedMilhares = new Set();

  3790: 
  3791:   function pickDiversifiedMilharesForDezena(dz, limit) {
  3792:     const candidates = ranked
  3793:       .filter((x) => x.dezena === dz && !usedMilhares.has(x.milhar))
  3794:       .map((x) => {
  3795:         const milhar = String(x.milhar || "").padStart(4, "0").slice(-4);
  3796:         const prefix = milhar.slice(0, 1);
  3797:         const centena = milhar.slice(1);
  3798:         const centenaScore = Number(centenaScoreMap.get(centena) || 0);
  3799: 
  3800:         return {
  3801:           ...x,
  3802:           milhar,
  3803:           prefix,
  3804:           centena,
  3805:           centenaScore,
  3806:           adjustedScore:
  3807:             centenaScore * 2 +
  3808:             Number(x.score || 0) -
  3809:             (prefix === "0" ? 80 : 0),
  3810:         };
  3811:       });
  3812: 
  3813:     const byCentena = new Map();
  3814: 
  3815:     for (const item of candidates) {
  3816:       if (!byCentena.has(item.centena)) byCentena.set(item.centena, []);
  3817:       byCentena.get(item.centena).push(item);
  3818:     }
  3819: 
  3820:     const centenaGroups = Array.from(byCentena.entries())
  3821:       .map(([centena, items]) => {
  3822:         const sortedItems = items.slice().sort((a, b) => {
  3823:           if (Number(b.adjustedScore) !== Number(a.adjustedScore)) {
  3824:             return Number(b.adjustedScore) - Number(a.adjustedScore);
  3825:           }
  3826:           if (Number(b.score) !== Number(a.score)) return Number(b.score) - Number(a.score);
  3827:           if (Number(b.targetHits) !== Number(a.targetHits)) return Number(b.targetHits) - Number(a.targetHits);
  3828:           if (Number(b.freq) !== Number(a.freq)) return Number(b.freq) - Number(a.freq);
  3829:           return milharCompareAsc(a.milhar, b.milhar);
  3830:         });
  3831: 
  3832:         const top = sortedItems[0] || {};
  3833: 
  3834:         return {
  3835:           centena,
  3836:           centenaScore: Number(top.centenaScore || 0),
  3837:           bestScore: Number(top.adjustedScore || 0),
  3838:           items: sortedItems,
  3839:         };
  3840:       })
  3841:       .sort((a, b) => {
  3842:         if (Number(b.centenaScore) !== Number(a.centenaScore)) {
  3843:           return Number(b.centenaScore) - Number(a.centenaScore);
  3844:         }
  3845:         if (Number(b.bestScore) !== Number(a.bestScore)) {
  3846:           return Number(b.bestScore) - Number(a.bestScore);
  3847:         }
  3848:         return String(a.centena).localeCompare(String(b.centena));
  3849:       });
  3850: 
  3851:     const picked = [];
  3852:     const usedPrefixes = new Set();
  3853:     const usedCentenas = new Set();
  3854: 
  3855:     // Fase 1: uma melhor milhar por centena forte, evitando repetir prefixo.
  3856:     for (const group of centenaGroups) {
  3857:       if (picked.length >= limit) break;
  3858: 
  3859:       const item = group.items.find((x) => !usedPrefixes.has(x.prefix));
  3860:       if (!item) continue;
  3861: 
  3862:       picked.push(item);
  3863:       usedPrefixes.add(item.prefix);
  3864:       usedCentenas.add(item.centena);
  3865:     }
  3866: 
  3867:     // Fase 2: se faltar, pega novas centenas fortes mesmo repetindo prefixo.
  3868:     for (const group of centenaGroups) {
  3869:       if (picked.length >= limit) break;
  3870:       if (usedCentenas.has(group.centena)) continue;
  3871: 
  3872:       const item = group.items.find((x) => !picked.some((p) => p.milhar === x.milhar));
  3873:       if (!item) continue;
  3874: 
  3875:       picked.push(item);
  3876:       usedPrefixes.add(item.prefix);
  3877:       usedCentenas.add(item.centena);
  3878:     }
  3879: 
  3880:     // Fase 3: se ainda faltar, permite mais de uma milhar da mesma centena.
  3881:     for (const group of centenaGroups) {
  3882:       if (picked.length >= limit) break;
  3883: 
  3884:       for (const item of group.items) {
  3885:         if (picked.length >= limit) break;
  3886:         if (picked.some((p) => p.milhar === item.milhar)) continue;
  3887: 
  3888:         picked.push(item);
  3889:       }
  3890:     }
  3891: 
  3892:     return picked.slice(0, limit);
  3893:   }

  3898:     let pushed = 0;
  3899: 
  3900:     for (const item of items) {
  3901:       usedMilhares.add(item.milhar);

  3878:     }
  3879: 
  3880:     // Fase 3: se ainda faltar, permite mais de uma milhar da mesma centena.
  3881:     for (const group of centenaGroups) {
  3882:       if (picked.length >= limit) break;
  3883: 
  3884:       for (const item of group.items) {
  3885:         if (picked.length >= limit) break;
  3886:         if (picked.some((p) => p.milhar === item.milhar)) continue;
  3887: 
  3888:         picked.push(item);
  3889:       }
  3890:     }
  3891: 
  3892:     return picked.slice(0, limit);
  3893:   }
  3894: 
  3895:   for (const dz of dezenasFixas) {
  3896:     const items = pickDiversifiedMilharesForDezena(dz, perDezena);
  3897: 
  3898:     let pushed = 0;
  3899: 
  3900:     for (const item of items) {
  3901:       usedMilhares.add(item.milhar);
  3902:       slots.push({
  3903:         dezena: dz,
  3904:         milhar: item.milhar,
  3905:         score: item.score,
  3906:         adjustedScore: item.adjustedScore,
  3907:         centena: item.centena,
  3908:         centenaScore: item.centenaScore,
  3909:         freq: item.freq,
  3910:         targetHits: item.targetHits,
  3911:         scheduleHits: item.scheduleHits,
  3912:         sameDowHits: item.sameDowHits,
  3913:         firstPrizeHits: item.firstPrizeHits,
  3914:       });
  3915:       pushed += 1;
  3916:     }
  3917: 
  3918:     while (pushed < perDezena) {
  3919:       slots.push({ dezena: dz, milhar: "" });
  3920:       pushed += 1;
  3921:     }
  3922:   }
  3923: 
  3924:   while (slots.length < N) slots.push({ dezena: "", milhar: "" });
  3925: 
  3926:   return { dezenas: dezenasFixas, slots: slots.slice(0, N) };
  3927: }

  3796:         const prefix = milhar.slice(0, 1);

  3845:         if (Number(b.bestScore) !== Number(a.bestScore)) {

  3853:     const usedCentenas = new Set();

  3855:     // Fase 1: uma melhor milhar por centena forte, evitando repetir prefixo.
  3856:     for (const group of centenaGroups) {
  3857:       if (picked.length >= limit) break;

  3877:       usedCentenas.add(item.centena);
  3878:     }

  3913:         firstPrizeHits: item.firstPrizeHits,
  3914:       });
  3915:       pushed += 1;
  3916:     }
  3917: 
  3918:     while (pushed < perDezena) {
  3919:       slots.push({ dezena: dz, milhar: "" });
  3920:       pushed += 1;
  3921:     }
  3922:   }
  3923: 
  3924:   while (slots.length < N) slots.push({ dezena: "", milhar: "" });
  3925:

  3927: }

  4001:   function evaluateSlot(slot) {

  4003:       .slice(0, 3)

  4017:       Number.isFinite(resultGrupo) &&
  4018:       Number(picks[0]) === resultGrupo;

  4023:         (grupo) => Number(grupo) === resultGrupo
  4024:       );

  4029:         picks.some(

  4035:       resultTop3.some(

  4038:           Number(result) === Number(grupo)
  4039:       )

  4042:     const matchedPrizePositions =
  4043:       prizePositionHits.filter(Boolean).length;
  4044: 
  4045:     const matchedPredictions =
  4046:       predictionHits.filter(Boolean).length;
  4047:

  4049:       picks,

  4051:       resultGrupo,
  4052:

  4055:

  4057:       prize2Hit: Boolean(prizePositionHits[1]),
  4058:       prize3Hit: Boolean(prizePositionHits[2]),

  3793:       .filter((x) => x.dezena === dz && !usedMilhares.has(x.milhar))

  3837:           bestScore: Number(top.adjustedScore || 0),

  3943: export function auditTop3Timeline({
  3944:   timeline,
  3945:   lotteryKey = "",
  3946: }) {
  3947:   const rows = Array.isArray(timeline) ? timeline : [];
  3948:

  3958:   function pct(n, d) {
  3959:     if (!d) return 0;
  3960: 
  3961:     return Number(
  3962:       ((Number(n || 0) / Number(d || 1)) * 100).toFixed(2)
  3963:     );
  3964:   }

  3967:     const source = Array.isArray(slot?.resultTop3Groups)
  3968:       ? slot.resultTop3Groups
  3969:       : [];
  3970: 
  3971:     const normalized = source
  3972:       .slice(0, 3)

  3974:         const value = Number(grupo);
  3975: 
  3976:         return Number.isFinite(value) &&
  3977:           value >= 1 &&
  3978:           value <= TOP3_GROUPS_K
  3979:           ? value
  3980:           : null;
  3981:       });
  3982: 
  3983:     while (normalized.length < 3) {
  3984:       normalized.push(null);
  3985:     }
  3986: 
  3987:     if (!Number.isFinite(Number(normalized[0]))) {
  3988:       const fallback = Number(slot?.resultGrupo);
  3989: 
  3990:       normalized[0] =
  3991:         Number.isFinite(fallback) &&
  3992:         fallback >= 1 &&
  3993:         fallback <= TOP3_GROUPS_K
  3994:           ? fallback
  3995:           : null;
  3996:     }
  3997: 
  3998:     return normalized;
  3999:   }
  4000: 
  4001:   function evaluateSlot(slot) {
  4002:     const picks = (Array.isArray(slot?.top3) ? slot.top3 : [])
  4003:       .slice(0, 3)
  4004:       .map((item) => Number(item?.grupo))
  4005:       .filter(
  4006:         (grupo) =>
  4007:           Number.isFinite(grupo) &&
  4008:           grupo >= 1 &&
  4009:           grupo <= TOP3_GROUPS_K

  4015:     const top1Hit =
  4016:       picks.length > 0 &&
  4017:       Number.isFinite(resultGrupo) &&
  4018:       Number(picks[0]) === resultGrupo;
  4019: 
  4020:     const top3Hit =
  4021:       Number.isFinite(resultGrupo) &&
  4022:       picks.some(
  4023:         (grupo) => Number(grupo) === resultGrupo
  4024:       );
  4025:

  4028:         Number.isFinite(Number(grupo)) &&

  4039:       )
  4040:     );
  4041: 
  4042:     const matchedPrizePositions =
  4043:       prizePositionHits.filter(Boolean).length;
  4044: 
  4045:     const matchedPredictions =
  4046:       predictionHits.filter(Boolean).length;
  4047: 
  4048:     return {
  4049:       picks,
  4050:       resultTop3,
  4051:       resultGrupo,
  4052: 
  4053:       top1Hit,
  4054:       top3Hit,
  4055: 
  4056:       prize1Hit: Boolean(prizePositionHits[0]),
  4057:       prize2Hit: Boolean(prizePositionHits[1]),
  4058:       prize3Hit: Boolean(prizePositionHits[2]),
  4059: 
  4060:       top3PrizeHit: matchedPrizePositions > 0,
  4061: 
  4062:       matchedPrizePositions,
  4063:       matchedPredictions,
  4064: 
  4065:       prizePositionHits,
  4066:       predictionHits,
  4067:     };
  4068:   }
  4069: 
  4070:   const evaluated = validated.map((slot) => ({
  4071:     slot,
  4072:     metrics: evaluateSlot(slot),
  4073:   }));

  3706:         inc += 1;

  3738:       };
  3739:     })
  3740:     .sort((a, b) => {
  3741:       if (Number(b.score) !== Number(a.score)) return Number(b.score) - Number(a.score);
  3742:       if (Number(b.targetHits) !== Number(a.targetHits)) return Number(b.targetHits) - Number(a.targetHits);
  3743:       if (Number(b.freq) !== Number(a.freq)) return Number(b.freq) - Number(a.freq);

  3796:         const prefix = milhar.slice(0, 1);
  3797:         const centena = milhar.slice(1);

  3807:             centenaScore * 2 +

  3809:             (prefix === "0" ? 80 : 0),
  3810:         };
  3811:       });
